The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">1</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the expected number of rolls showing face 5 under the null hypothesis?</p>	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">8</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> &asymp; <strong>1.33</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">6</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">1</sub></span> = <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">54</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> = <strong>9</strong>	Correct	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">54</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">5</sub></span> = <strong>10.8</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">54</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">1</sub></span> = <strong>54</strong>	Incorrect

The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">1</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the expected number of rolls showing face 1 under the null hypothesis?</p>	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">1</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> &asymp; <strong>0.17</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">84</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6<sup>2</sup></sub></span> &asymp; <strong>2.33</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">30</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> = <strong>5</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">6</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">1</sub></span> = <strong>6</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">84</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> = <strong>14</strong>	Correct
